</ul>
<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>PostgreSQL durability:</strong> Honor the server’s synchronous_commit setting instead of forcing it off, while keeping port and backup transactions durable. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5984197411" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180821" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180821/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180821">#180821</a> Authored by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/steipete/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/steipete">@steipete</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Delegated administration:</strong> Require approval for persistent delegated changes without effective authority, support explicit global or per-agent standing policy, and recheck the grant at each persistent effect. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6320277740" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/196984" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196984/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196984">#196984</a> Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Input policy hooks:</strong> Expose prepared current-turn images to before_agent_run policy hooks ahead of model submission, using the same image bytes once approved. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6297432854" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/196337" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196337/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196337">#196337</a> Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Diagnostics privacy:</strong> Redact multiline captured telemetry and identifying triage-map keys. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6251998247" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/194353" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194353/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194353">#194353</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5895900081" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175272" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175272/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175272">#175272</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5891192082" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174680" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174680/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174680">#174680</a> Authored by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/steipete/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/steipete">@steipete</a>. <h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>PostgreSQL durability:</strong> Honor the server’s synchronous_commit setting instead of forcing it off, while keeping port and backup transactions durable. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5984197411" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180821" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180821/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180821">#180821</a> Authored by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/steipete/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/steipete">@steipete</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Delegated administration:</strong> Require approval for persistent delegated changes without effective authority, support explicit global or per-agent standing policy, and recheck the grant at each persistent effect. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6320277740" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/196984" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196984/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196984">#196984</a> Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Input policy hooks:</strong> Expose prepared current-turn images to before_agent_run policy hooks ahead of model submission, using the same image bytes once approved. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6297432854" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/196337" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196337/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196337">#196337</a> Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Diagnostics privacy:</strong> Redact multiline captured telemetry and identifying triage-map keys. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6251998247" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/194353" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194353/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194353">#194353</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5895900081" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175272" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175272/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175272">#175272</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5891192082" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174680" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174680/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/174680">#174680</a> Authored by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/steipete/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/steipete">@steipete</a>. <li><strong>iOS pairing privacy:</strong> Stop persisting pasted setup codes on the device. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6322409624" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/197049" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197049/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197049">#197049</a> Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li><strong>IRC sender policy:</strong> Treat an explicitly empty channel sender list as deny-all rather than falling back to broader sender access. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6296103480" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/196293" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196293/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196293">#196293</a> Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/mmaps/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/mmaps">@mmaps</a>.</li>
